# Common Security Services Manager (Swift)

**Framework:** Security  
**Kind:** API Collection

A set of open source modules underpinning the legacy implementation of the Security framework.

# Common Security Services Manager (Objective-C)

**Framework:** Security  
**Kind:** API Collection

A set of open source modules underpinning the legacy implementation of the Security framework.
